
> mothership-workspace@0.1.0 verify
> npm run check:toolchain && npm run check:workspace && npm run check:sources && npm run typecheck && npm test && npm run test:balance && npm run test:scenarios && npm run test:capacity


> mothership-workspace@0.1.0 check:toolchain
> node scripts/check-toolchain.mjs

Toolchain: Node 22.21.1, npm 10.9.4

> mothership-workspace@0.1.0 check:workspace
> node scripts/check-workspace.mjs

Workspace boundaries: 8 explicit packages; TS/TSX imports checked; pure and browser programs separated; exact runtime subpaths; fixture and reference imports excluded

> mothership-workspace@0.1.0 check:sources
> node scripts/check-sources.mjs

Board V2 exact runtime ports: 27 pinned source/derivative pairs; runtime adoption is reviewed separately
Reviewed common setup/motion art: 25 exact SVGs
Source integrity: 119 original Canvas files + 1 unbound example; 7 rule sources; 24 pinned source files; token proposal unchanged
Approved V1 source integrity: 21 immutable owner decisions; historical source pin preserved
Capacity owner source integrity: six confirmed decisions; separate exact behavior tuple; historical bytes preserved
Capacity resolution integrity: six latest owner decisions; exact historical tuples preserved
Vote result integrity: approved five-second checkpoints and exact new tuple bound
Declared Hack integrity: approved claim, bounded flow and exact historical tuple preservation
Public Hack draft integrity: public pair and approved turn-stage movement isolated to exact new tuple; creation defaults unchanged
Board/medical draft integrity: final owner slots and episode rules bound to separate inactive tuple; historical pins preserved
Captain outcome candidate integrity: exact successor; historical source and activation pins retained
Blackout candidate integrity: exact fixed-window successor and private room outcome boundary
Clone candidate integrity: exact lifetime/normal-round successor; historical pins and defaults retained
Planet art integrity: 17 common stylesheets and 5 approved film pairs; historical releases preserved
Crew art registration: 17 exact ports; 3 new uniform bundles; tokens 0.5.0; runtime activation remains separate
Pregame art registration: 22 exact named modules; frozen dependencies; consumer review and setup flow activation remain separate
